data-pm=\"normal\" data-sstype=\"layer\"><div class=\"n2-ss-item-content n2-ss-text n2-ow-all\"><div class=\"\"><p class=\"n2-font-b622a6882ad1de5a7cdee8babcd88712-paragraph   \">Bringing daylight into your home has never been simpler. At TINTEK Roofing &amp; Cladding, we install a wide range of innovative Solatube daylighting and ventilation solutions that keep your rooms bright, comfortable, and energy-efficient all year round.<br>\n‎<br>\nSolatube’s tubular skylight technology is designed to capture and reflect the maximum amount of natural light, delivering a clean, high-quality illumination into any space. Available in two sizes, these skylights can be tailored to suit everything from small hallways to large living areas.<br>\n‎<br>\nA Solatube system will fill your home with beautiful natural light while blocking unwanted UV rays and excess heat, protecting your interiors and keeping your home cooler.<br>\n‎<br>\nFor extra convenience, you can upgrade to the Solar Powered Daylight Control System, giving you full control over how much daylight enters the room and automatically closing at night for total comfort.</p>\n</div></div></div><div class=\"n2-ss-layer n2-ow n-uc-YqHv1cmHkS6M\"
